<h2>History by venue</h2>
<p class="gm-method">The full held history for each venue, in the venue's own unit. Series longer than 280 reports print as yearly extremes over the full span plus the most recent 104 reports; shorter series print in full. Every table sorts and filters.</p>
<h3>CFTC Disaggregated, COPPER- #1: full held history, Disaggregated categories, contracts</h3>

<p class="gm-method">Bank positions are facilitation and hedge books reported on Form 40, not proprietary views. A bank count under four is suppressed by the CFTC and prints as suppressed, never zero.</p>

<tr><td>Price window</td><td>The site's market tables hold about one month of daily observations; no longer price history is held for this commodity.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Position and publication dates</td><td>Position dates print per venue above; this price is the latest market-table value and is not aligned to any position date.</td></tr>

<p class="gm-method">The price panel prints only where the site already holds the series, and it is not merged with the positioning panels: positioning is dated Tuesday or Friday and published later, so the two series are not contemporaneous.</p>

<h2>Coverage and method</h2>
<p class="gm-method">The ESMA LME line ends in December 2020 at Brexit. The Shanghai leg joins when the SHFE dataset lands.</p>
<p class="gm-method">Sector: Metals, Base metals. Units print per row and are never converted. Category definitions are each source's own. Pages render the current corrected vintage of each source; first-release vintages are not kept. An extreme reading is a condition of crowding, not a buy or sell signal.</p>
